   01081:

   01161: function computeStats({ drawsRawView, drawsRawGlobal, filters, selectedGrupo }) {
   01162:   const drawsView = dedupeDraws(drawsRawView);
   01163:   const drawsGlobal = dedupeDraws(drawsRawGlobal);
   01164: 
   01165:   const gSel = Number(selectedGrupo);
   01166:   const hasSelected = Number.isFinite(gSel) && gSel >= 1 && gSel <= 25;
   01167: 
>> 01168:   const expectedPositions = parseExpectedPositionsFromFilters(filters);
   01169:   const selectedPosOrNull = normalizePosFilter(filters);
   01170: 
   01171:   const draws = hasSelected
   01172:     ? drawsView.filter((d) => drawHasGrupo(d, gSel))
   01173:     : drawsView;
   01174: 
   01175:   const yearsSet = new Set();
   01176:   for (const d of draws) {
   01177:     const ymd = getDrawDate(d);
   01178:     if (!ymd) continue;
   01179:     const y = Number(ymd.slice(0, 4));
   01180:     if (Number.isFinite(y) && y > 1900) yearsSet.add(y);
   01181:   }
   01182:   const isMultiYear = yearsSet.size > 1;
   01183: 
   01184:   const monthOrder = [
   01185:     "Janeiro",
   01186:     "Fevereiro",
   01187:     "Março",
   01188:     "Abril",
   01189:     "Maio",
   01190:     "Junho",
   01191:     "Julho",
   01192:     "Agosto",
   01193:     "Setembro",
   01194:     "Outubro",
   01195:     "Novembro",
   01196:     "Dezembro",
   01197:   ];
   01198: 
   01199:   let totalAparicoesNoRecorte = 0;
   01200:   for (const d of draws) {
   01201:     totalAparicoesNoRecorte += countAparicoesInDraw(d, {
   01202:       selectedGrupo: hasSelected ? gSel : null,
   01203:       selectedPosOrNull,
>> 01204:       expectedPositions,
   01205:     });
   01206:   }
   01207: 
   01208:   const monthMap = new Map();
   01209:   for (const d of draws) {
   01210:     const ymd = getDrawDate(d);
   01211:     const m = ymd ? getMonthPT(ymd) : null;
   01212:     if (!m) continue;
   01213: 
   01214:     const apar = countAparicoesInDraw(d, {
   01215:       selectedGrupo: hasSelected ? gSel : null,
   01216:       selectedPosOrNull,
>> 01217:       expectedPositions,
   01218:     });
   01219: 
   01220:     monthMap.set(m, (monthMap.get(m) || 0) + apar);
   01221:   }
   01222: 
   01223:   const monthDataDisplay = monthOrder.map((m) => ({
   01224:     label: m,
   01225:     value: monthMap.get(m) || 0,
   01226:   }));
   01227: 
   01228:   const wdOrder = [
   01229:     { key: "Dom", label: "domingo" },
   01230:     { key: "Seg", label: "segunda-feira" },
   01231:     { key: "Ter", label: "terça-feira" },
   01232:     { key: "Qua", label: "quarta-feira" },
   01233:     { key: "Qui", label: "quinta-feira" },
   01234:     { key: "Sex", label: "sexta-feira" },
   01235:     { key: "Sáb", label: "sábado" },
   01236:   ];
   01237: 
   01238:   const wdMap = new Map(wdOrder.map((k) => [k.key, 0]));
   01239: 
   01240:   for (const d of draws) {
   01241:     const ymd = getDrawDate(d);
   01242:     if (!ymd) continue;
   01243: 
   01244:     const [y, m, dd] = ymd.split("-").map(Number);
   01245:     if (!y || !m || !dd) continue;
   01246: 
   01247:     const dt = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, dd));
   01248:     const w = dt.getUTCDay();
   01249:     const map = ["Dom", "Seg", "Ter", "Qua", "Qui", "Sex", "Sáb"];
   01250:     const wd = map[w] || null;
   01251:     if (!wd) continue;
   01252: 
   01253:     const apar = countAparicoesInDraw(d, {
   01254:       selectedGrupo: hasSelected ? gSel : null,
   01255:       selectedPosOrNull,
>> 01256:       expectedPositions,
   01257:     });
   01258: 
   01259:     wdMap.set(wd, (wdMap.get(wd) || 0) + apar);
   01260:   }
   01261: 
>> 01262:   const weekdayData = wdOrder.map((it) => ({
   01263:     label: it.label,
   01264:     value: wdMap.get(it.key) || 0,
   01265:   }));
   01266: 
   01267:   const ALLOWED_BUCKETS = ["09h", "11h", "14h", "16h", "18h", "21h"];
   01268:   const hourMap = new Map(ALLOWED_BUCKETS.map((b) => [b, 0]));
   01269:   let aparicoesComHoraTotal = 0;
   01270: 
   01271:   for (const d of draws) {
   01272:     const bucket = hhmmToBucket09h(getDrawCloseHour(d));
   01273:     if (!bucket) continue;
   01274:     if (!hourMap.has(bucket)) continue;
   01275: 
   01276:     const apar = countAparicoesInDraw(d, {
   01277:       selectedGrupo: hasSelected ? gSel : null,
   01278:       selectedPosOrNull,
>> 01279:       expectedPositions,
   01280:     });
   01281: 
   01282:     hourMap.set(bucket, (hourMap.get(bucket) || 0) + apar);
   01283:     aparicoesComHoraTotal += apar;
   01284:   }
   01285: 
   01286:   const hourData = ALLOWED_BUCKETS.map((label) => ({
   01287:     label,
   01288:     value: hourMap.get(label) || 0,
   01289:   })).filter((x) => safeNumber(x.value) > 0);
   01290: 
   01291:   const hourDataWithTotal =
   01292:     hourData.length > 0 && aparicoesComHoraTotal > 0
   01293:       ? [...hourData, { label: "Total", value: aparicoesComHoraTotal }]
   01294:       : [];
   01295: 
   01296:   let posRankingModel = null;
   01297:   let selectedAnimalLabel = null;
   01298: 
   01299:   if (hasSelected) {
   01300:     try {
   01301:       selectedAnimalLabel = String(
   01302:         getAnimalLabel({ grupo: gSel, animal: "" }) || ""
   01303:       ).trim();
   01304:     } catch {
   01305:       selectedAnimalLabel = "";
   01306:     }
   01307:     if (!selectedAnimalLabel)
   01308:       selectedAnimalLabel = `Grupo ${String(gSel).padStart(2, "0")}`;
   01309: 
>> 01310:     const grpCount = countGrupoAparicoes(draws, gSel, expectedPositions);
   01311: 
   01312:     posRankingModel = {
   01313:       mode: "dist",
>> 01314:       header: "Aparições do animal por posição",
   01315:       animalLabel: String(selectedAnimalLabel || "").trim(),
   01316:       items: grpCount.byPos,
   01317:     };
   01318:   } else {
   01319:     posRankingModel = buildPosAnimalRanking(
   01320:       drawsView,
>> 01321:       expectedPositions,
   01322:       selectedPosOrNull
   01323:     );
   01324:   }
   01325: 
   01326:   const aparicoesGlobal25 = buildGlobalAparicoes25(drawsGlobal);
   01327: 
   01328:   return {
   01329:     totalAparicoesNoRecorte,
   01330:     isMultiYear,
   01331:     monthDataDisplay,
>> 01332:     weekdayData,
   01333:     hourData: hourDataWithTotal,
>> 01334:     expectedPositions,
   01335:     selectedPosOrNull,
   01336:     posRankingModel,
   01337:     hasSelected,
   01338:     selectedAnimalLabel,
   01339:     aparicoesGlobal25,
   01340:   };
   01341: }

   00033: function normalizeValue(v, fallback = "Todos") {
   00034:   const s = String(v ?? "").trim();
   00035:   return s ? s : fallback;
   00036: }
   00037: 
   00038: function sortPTBR(a, b) {
   00039:   return String(a).localeCompare(String(b), "pt-BR", { sensitivity: "base" });
   00040: }
   00041: 
   00042: function normalizeLoteriaInput(v) {
   00043:   const raw = String(v ?? "").trim();
   00044:   if (!raw) return "PT_RIO";
   00045: 
   00046:   const key = raw
   00047:     .toLowerCase()
   00048:     .normalize("NFD")
   00049:     .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "")
   00050:     .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
   00051:     .trim();
   00052: 
   00053:   if (key === "federal" || key === "fed" || key === "br" || key === "brasil")
   00054:     return "FEDERAL";
   00055:   if (key === "rj" || key === "rio" || key === "pt_rio" || key === "pt-rio")
   00056:     return "PT_RIO";
   00057: 
   00058:   const out = key
   00059:     .toUpperCase()
   00060:     .replace(/[^A-Z0-9]+/g, "_")
   00061:     .replace(/^_+|_+$/g, "");
   00062: 
   00063:   return out || "PT_RIO";
   00064: }
   00065: 
>> 00066: /** Normaliza posição para "1º..7º" ou "Todos" */
   00067: function normalizePosicaoInput(v) {
   00068:   const s = String(v ?? "").trim();
   00069:   if (!s) return "Todos";
   00070:   if (s.toLowerCase() === "todos") return "Todos";
   00071: 
   00072:   const mNum = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})$/);
   00073:   if (mNum) return `${Number(mNum[1])}º`;
   00074: 
   00075:   const mOrd = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})\s*º$/);
   00076:   if (mOrd) return `${Number(mOrd[1])}º`;
   00077: 
   00078:   return s;
   00079: }

   00068: function drawQualityScore(draw) {
   00069:   const prizes = Array.isArray(draw?.prizes) ? draw.prizes : [];
   00070:   const y = pickDrawYMD(draw);
   00071:   const h = toHourBucket(pickDrawHour(draw));
   00072: 
   00073:   let score = 0;
   00074:   if (isYMD(y)) score += 20;
   00075:   if (h) score += 20;
   00076:   score += Math.min(10, prizes.length) * 10;
   00077: 
   00078:   const hasP1 = prizes.some((p) => guessPrizePos(p) === 1);
   00079:   if (hasP1) score += 15;
   00080: 
   00081:   return score;
   00082: }

   00068: function normalizeMilhar(value) {
   00069:   const digits = String(value || "").replace(/\D/g, "");

   00044: function dayDiffInclusiveUTC(ymdFrom, ymdTo) {
   00045:   const toUTC = (ymd) => {
   00046:     const m = String(ymd).match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
   00047:     if (!m) return null;
   00048:     return new Date(Date.UTC(Number(m[1]), Number(m[2]) - 1, Number(m[3])));
   00049:   };
   00050: 
   00051:   const da = toUTC(ymdFrom);
   00052:   const db = toUTC(ymdTo);
   00053:   if (!da || !db) return NaN;
   00054: 
   00055:   const ms = db.getTime() - da.getTime();
   00056:   if (!Number.isFinite(ms)) return NaN;
   00057: 
   00058:   return Math.floor(ms / 86400000) + 1;
   00059: }
   00060: 
>> 00061: function normalizePositionsFromKey(positionsInputKey) {
>> 00062:   const key = String(positionsInputKey || "").trim();
   00063:   if (!key) return null;
   00064: 
   00065:   const nums = key
   00066:     .split(",")
   00067:     .map((x) => Number(String(x).trim()))
   00068:     .filter((n) => Number.isFinite(n));
   00069: 
   00070:   if (!nums.length) return null;
   00071:   return Array.from(new Set(nums)).sort((a, b) => a - b);
   00072: }
   00073: 
   00074: function normHHMM(value) {
   00075:   const s = String(value || "").trim();
   00076:   if (!s) return "";
   00077: 
   00078:   const mh = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})h$/i);
   00079:   if (mh) return `${String(mh[1]).padStart(2, "0")}:00`;
   00080:

   00306:   for (const c of candidates) {

   00312:   }

   00316: /* ========= chunking ========= */
   00317: // default seguro (mesmo número que você usa no view)
   00318: const DEFAULT_CHUNK_DAYS = 45;
   00319: 
   00320: export function splitRangeIntoChunks(fromYmd, toYmd, chunkDays = DEFAULT_CHUNK_DAYS) {
   00321:   const out = [];
   00322:   if (!isYMD(fromYmd) || !isYMD(toYmd)) return out;
   00323:   if (fromYmd > toYmd) return out;
   00324: 
   00325:   let curFrom = fromYmd;
   00326:   while (curFrom <= toYmd) {
   00327:     const curTo = addDaysUTC(curFrom, Number(chunkDays || DEFAULT_CHUNK_DAYS) - 1);
   00328:     const boundedTo = curTo > toYmd ? toYmd : curTo;
   00329:     out.push({ from: curFrom, to: boundedTo });
   00330:     const next = addDaysUTC(boundedTo, 1);
   00331:     if (next <= curFrom) break;
   00332:     curFrom = next;
   00333:   }
   00334:   return out;

   00313:       ([key, value]) => [key, value / total]
   00314:     )
   00315:   );
   00316: }

   00399:

   00402:

>> 00404:   let invalidPositionCount = 0;
   00405:

   00407:     const prizes = Array.isArray(d?.prizes) ? d.prizes : [];
   00408:

   00410:     else drawsWithoutPrizes += 1;

   00413:     if (!ymd) invalidDateCount += 1;

   00416:     if (!hhmm || !isValidHHMM(hhmm)) invalidCloseHourCount += 1;
   00417:

   00419:

   00423:         missingGrupoCount += 1;

   00425:         invalidGrupoCount += 1;
   00426:       }
   00427:

   00433:         if (!Number.isFinite(pos) || pos < 1 || pos > 10) {
>> 00434:           invalidPositionCount += 1;
   00435:         }
   00436:       }
   00437:     }
   00438:   }
   00439: 
   00440:   const byGrupo = auditCountsByGrupo(draws, {

   00442:     hour: options?.hour ?? null,
   00443:     matchHourOnly: options?.matchHourOnly ?? false,
   00444:   });
   00445: 
   00446:   const animalMismatchCount = byGrupo.integrityIssues.length;
   00447:   const integrityIssuesSample = byGrupo.integrityIssues.slice(
   00448:     0,
   00449:     Math.max(0, Number.isFinite(sampleLimit) ? sampleLimit : 20)
   00450:   );

   00460:       prizesTotal,

>> 00007: export function normalizePositions(positions) {
   00008:   const arr =
>> 00009:     Array.isArray(positions) && positions.length
>> 00010:       ? positions
   00011:           .map(Number)
   00012:           .filter((n) => Number.isFinite(n) && n > 0)
   00013:       : null;
   00014: 
   00015:   if (!arr || !arr.length) return null;
   00016:   return Array.from(new Set(arr)).sort((a, b) => a - b);
   00017: }
   00018: 
   00019: export function isValidGrupo(n) {
   00020:   return Number.isFinite(Number(n)) && Number(n) >= 1 && Number(n) <= 25;
   00021: }
   00022: 
>> 00023: export function isValidPosition(n) {
   00024:   return Number.isFinite(Number(n)) && Number(n) >= 1 && Number(n) <= 10;
   00025: }
   00026: 
   00027: export function extractIntInRange(value, min, max) {
   00028:   if (value === null || value === undefined) return null;
   00029: 
   00030:   if (typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value)) {
   00031:     const n = Math.trunc(value);
   00032:     return n >= min && n <= max ? n : null;
   00033:   }
   00034: 
   00035:   const s = String(value).trim();
   00036:   if (!s) return null;
   00037: 
   00038:   if (/^\d+$/.test(s)) {
   00039:     const n = Number(s);
   00040:     return Number.isFinite(n) && n >= min && n <= max ? n : null;
   00041:   }
   00042: 
   00043:   const m = s.match(/(\d{1,2})/);
   00044:   if (!m) return null;
   00045:   const n = Number(m[1]);
   00046:   return Number.isFinite(n) && n >= min && n <= max ? n : null;
   00047: }
   00048: 
   00049: export function normalizeDigitsOnly(v) {
   00050:   const s = String(v ?? "").trim();
   00051:   if (!s) return "";
   00052:   return s.replace(/\D+/g, "");
   00053: }
   00054: 
>> 00055: export function prizeWidthByPosition(position) {
>> 00056:   return Number(position) === 7 ? 3 : 4;
   00057: }
   00058: 
>> 00059: export function toPrizeDigitsByPosition(input, position) {
   00060:   const digits = normalizeDigitsOnly(input);
   00061:   if (!digits) return null;
   00062: 
>> 00063:   const w = prizeWidthByPosition(position);
   00064: 
   00065:   if (w === 3) {
   00066:     const last3 = digits.slice(-3).padStart(3, "0");
   00067:     return /^\d{3}$/.test(last3) ? last3 : null;
   00068:   }
   00069: 
   00070:   const last4 = digits.slice(-4).padStart(4, "0");
   00071:   return /^\d{4}$/.test(last4) ? last4 : null;
   00072: }
   00073: 
   00074: export function digitsToDezena2(numStr) {
   00075:   const s = String(numStr || "");
   00076:   if (!/^\d{2,4}$/.test(s)) return null;
   00077:   return s.slice(-2);
   00078: }
   00079: 
   00080: export function digitsToCentena3(numStr) {
   00081:   const s = String(numStr || "");
   00082:   if (!/^\d{3,4}$/.test(s)) return null;
   00083:   return s.slice(-3);
   00084: }

   01021:     .sort();

   01562: function normalizeLotteriesList(lotteries) {

   00179: function dedupePrizes(prizes) {
   00180:   const arr = Array.isArray(prizes) ? prizes : [];
   00181:   if (!arr.length) return arr;
   00182: 
   00183:   const seen = new Set();
   00184:   const out = [];

   00190:     const animalRaw = p?.animal ?? "";
